PREFACE 


This brochure contains an index to the Gazette of India for the 
month of May, 1957. Notifications relating to Acts, Ordinances and 
Regulations (Part II, Section 1); Bills and Reports of Select Com¬ 
mittees on Bills (Part II, Section 2); and Statutory Rules and Orders 
(Part II, Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed exhaustively. As 
regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections 1 and 3, Part III, Action 
1) only items of importance from Members’ viewpoint have been 
indexed. References to the Parts and Sections of Gazette along with 
the page numbers have been given in the index for facility of 
reference. 


New Delhi; 

The 18th' November, 1957. 


M. N. KAUL, 
Secretary.

PREFACE 


This brochure contains an Index to the Gazette of India for the 
month of June, 1957. Notifications relating to Acts, Ordinances and 
Regulations (Part IT, Section 1) and Statutory Rules and Orders (Part 
II, Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed exhaustively. As regards 
other Notifications (part I. Sections 1 and 3, Part Ill, Section 1) only 
items of importance from Members’ viewpoint have been indexed. 
References to the Parts and Sections of Gazette along with the page 
numbers have been given in the index for facility of reference. 


New Delhi; 

The 3\st August, 1957 


M, N. KAUL, 

Secretary.

PREFACE 


This brochure contains an index to the Gazette of India for the 
month of July, 1957. Notifications relating to Acts, Ordinances and 
Regulations (Part TI, Section I); Bills and Reports of Select 
Committees on Bids (Part II, Section 2); and Statutory 
Rules and Orders (Part II, Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed exhaus¬ 
tively. As regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections 1 and 3, Part 
m, Section I) only items of importance from Members’ viewpoint have 
been indexed. Reference to the Parts and Sections of Gazette along 
with the page numbers have been given in the index for facility of 
reference. 


New Delhi; 

The \Qth December, 1957. 


M. N. KAUL, 
Secretary.
